# Next-wave Improvement Plan — 2026-09-28

## 最新驗收狀態（2026-10-05；以下歷史紀錄不覆蓋此表）

階段阻斷確認：多輪 ADB devices／mdns 查證均無裝置，已提出重接手機 USB 與更新手錶連接埠的需求，尚無回覆；沒有正在執行的掃描、建置或 AI 請求可等待。下一個必要關卡為安裝近期修正、工程讀取故障／背壓實機驗證及三輪配對掃描。未取得正常取數與保存基準前，不啟用正式 RawSensorStore 加密切換；其目前仍為 legacy prefs。這不代表所有程式已完成，亦不代表可以發布：正式加密／刪除競態、傳輸中斷、耗電與原金鑰簽章仍待驗收。為避免無裝置時反覆小修／重跑綠燈測試，目標進入等待外部條件的 blocked 狀態；恢復連線後續作同一完整目標。

工程頁讀取失敗空畫面分類補正：raw_snapshot_query 已記 failed，但 recent 為空時仍寫「尚無快照」，會把存取故障冒充無紀錄。改為成功查詢無通道、讀取失敗無法確認、尚未查詢三種文字；RawSnapshotDiagnostics 三項測試與手機 Debug 通過（raw-query-ui-classification-20261005.log）。未改感測值，未安裝或實機注入故障；仍等待裝置連線。

失敗記錄排序補驗：RawPersistenceOutcome 共三項測試通過，涵蓋舊成功不能清新拒收、舊失敗不能覆蓋新成功、重試待完成不可先隱藏前次失敗。raw_backpressure Android 隔離入口新增排空舊工作後拒收原因仍在、後續 commit 成功才清除的斷言；Debug 建置通過（raw-outcome-regression-20261005.log）。新增實機斷言仍未執行，已請使用者恢復手機 USB 與提供手錶最新無線偵錯連接埠。

加密寫入失敗記錄亂序修正：舊佇列工作成功原會無條件清除 lastPersistenceFailure，即使較新 apply 已拒收；新增 RawPersistenceOutcome 依操作序號更新，舊完成不可清掉新拒收，後續成功提交才清除。定向回歸與手機 Debug 通過（raw-outcome-ordering-20261005.log）；只驗記錄排序，Android 真實 executor 故障注入仍待重連，未啟用正式 raw 切換或安裝。

完整近期回歸已結束：paired-final-review-20261005.log BUILD SUCCESSFUL；手機 634／Wear 198 項 Debug 單元測試，failure/error=0；雙端完整 Release lint 與 Release 建置完成。警告不等於已全部修復，測試不代替實機；本批未安裝，正式 raw 切換、三輪配對驗收與原金鑰簽章仍未完成。

近期修正後的完整回歸／lint／Release 已啟動（paired-final-review-20261005.log），工具 session 47143 已確認存活；尚未结束不得沿用舊測試數字宣稱本批全部通過。重新查 SensorSuiteDump.pendingContext 賦值與 dispatchWrite，持有 applicationContext，未證實 StaticFieldLeak 警告代表 Activity 洩漏；不為消警告盲改。正式儲存、裝置掃描及簽章驗收仍未完成。

Health Connect 分頁容量溢位修正：records.size + page.records.size 的 Int 加法可溢位，改為比較剩餘容量；使用不配置巨量元素的 AbstractList 回歸確認第二頁報超大 size 時，在迭代／addAll 前拒絕整次結果。HealthConnectPageTokenTrackerTest 通過（hc-page-overflow-20261005.log），含原有循環 token、頁數界限與取消測試。這是防禦邊界，不宣稱 Health Connect 實機曾回報這種頁面。ADB devices 與 mdns services 均空，沒有可自動重連裝置，未安裝或掃描。

工程診斷一致快照修正：原先從 raw.all 取 keys 後逐欄 getLong／getInt／getString，同步更新可讓同一通道時間、樣本數與狀態混用不同版本。新增 RawSnapshotDiagnostics，僅解析單次 all 快照；保留合法 0、缺少 samples 維持 -1、status 維持 unknown，格式錯誤交由已隔離 raw_snapshot_query 回報，不把錯誤轉成 0。兩項回歸與 Debug 建置通過（raw-diagnostic-snapshot-20261005.log）；尚未安裝或實機故障注入。

Hermes 新審查已收回並核對，網路斷線與本機 executor 拒收沒有等價因果，不採用該建議作測試證明。工程頁 raw 讀取錯誤已隔離、取消仍傳播，新增 raw_snapshot_query 到 UI／匯出；移除 renderReport 無用途再次開啟 raw。Debug 建置通過（debug-storage-isolation-20261005.log）；未注入實機存取故障、未安裝，正式加密切換仍未啟用。細節 raw-cutover-callsite-audit-2026-10-05.md。

Health Connect 人工補水總量數值閘修正：acceptsAggregate 原只驗平台 aggregate，未驗 validatedRecordTotal；正無限記錄總量可讓差值與容差同為 infinity，誤通過比較；微小負總量亦可能落入容差。新增記錄總量 finite／nonnegative 檢查，保留合法 0，不以造值處理缺失。回歸涵蓋 NaN、正負無限及微小負值；aggregate／hydration 定向測試通過（hc-invalid-record-total-20261005.log）。尚未證明使用者真實補水紀錄含此錯誤，也未安裝或作實機資料驗收。

AI 協作實際重查：瀏覽器可列出 Grok／Claude／ChatGPT／Gemini／DeepSeek／Meta／Kimi 分頁；Grok 既有審查分頁兩次綁定均 CDP focus 逾時，未提交新問題或取得新回覆。不再把「無 Grokbot 本機端點」誤寫成沒有 Grok 網頁；分頁存在亦不等於代理已開工。地端查證紀錄存於 Obsidian responses/20261005-1200-xhb-codex-ai-access-audit.md；未傳健康資料或原始碼。ADB 清單仍空，實機驗收未推進。

Health Connect 區間稽核：跨日曆分桶使用 LocalDateTime／Period；既有測試涵蓋 23／25 小時日曆日，不把一天固定成 24 小時。另確認 completeIntervalsWithinRange 僅驗查詢邊界，未拒絕零長度或反向紀錄；現補上正區間要求，避免無效睡眠時段進入七日趨勢，倒置查詢明確失敗。新增回歸測試，定向 aggregate／calendar 測試通過（hc-interval-validity-20261005.log）。這是輸入邊界缺口，不宣稱已重現使用者缺值原因；實際授權後分桶／来源對照仍待連線驗收。

原始快照刪除邊界稽核：正式 LocalSensorDataDeletion 目前經 RawSensorStore 清空舊 prefs，尚未切換加密 registry；不能以此宣稱正式 encrypted snapshot 刪除已驗收。補上遷移 cleanup 拋例外回歸：保留已提交加密證據與舊資料，例外傳播而非回報刪除成功；再次執行可完成 cleanup。RawSnapshotMigrationTest 共 8 項通過（raw-cleanup-exception-20261005.log），只驗證協調器控制流程，不證明 Android 實際檔案刪除或多執行緒刪除／採集競態已處理。本批僅測試變更，未安裝。

最新集中回歸完成：手機 628、Wear 198 項 Debug 單元測試，failure/error 皆 0；雙端 assembleRelease 通過（paired-regression-20261005.log）。本批包含未知旗標顯示／匯出與 apply 拒收記錄修正；未重跑完整 lint。重新 apksigner verify 雙端 Release 都 DOES NOT VERIFY，artifact 仍 unsigned，不能發布。ADB 清單無裝置，本批未安裝、沒有新腕部量測；826 項測試不等於硬體成功率或完整臨床驗證。

加密儲存非同步拒收可觀測性修正：佇列已滿時，原 apply 拋出拒收但未更新 lastPersistenceFailure，診斷可能漏報；移至共用 enqueue 記錄，commit 維持 false、apply 維持拋出，不把拒收值發布到記憶體。隔離 raw_backpressure 案例增加 apply 拒收／舊值保持／失敗原因斷言。Debug 與 editor／transactions 定向測試通過（raw-apply-rejection-20261005.log）；這些 JVM 測試不涵蓋 Android executor 拒收，手機離線，新增實機斷言尚未執行，正式 RawSensorStore 未切換。

工程診斷缺失回報分類修正：未收到 watch report 時，原畫面把全掃描、健康 FGS、WakeLock 預設成 false，誤導成已確認未啟動；現在僅接受明確 Boolean，缺失或格式錯誤顯示未知。同步修正掃描命令說明及診斷 JSON 匯出，未知旗標輸出 null 而非 false。兩項回歸測試與手機 Debug 建置通過，最後日誌 observed-boolean-export-20261005.log。Hermes 本機匿名技術摘要審查請求 45 秒逾時，未取得回覆；Grokbot 尚無已驗證端點，不宣稱參與。33501 連線逾時且 ADB 清單無裝置，本批尚未安裝或實機驗收；不得把未知狀態當採集故障證據。

確認收件故障回歸：新增 DataItemDurabilityCommitTest，4 項定向測試通過，涵蓋 commit=false、提交例外、下游處理例外及重試需重新提交後才 ack（durability-ack-failures-20261005.log）。只證明共用 helper 的控制流程，不代替 Health Services 生產路徑磁碟故障或實際 Data Layer 重送驗收。Hermes 審查請求中斷後工具 handle 已不存在、回覆檔也未產生，不宣稱該輪收到意見。本批僅測試變更，未重新安裝。

Health Services 單筆心率持久化缺口修正：storeHealthServicesHeartRateSnapshot 原 feature／raw 寫入使用 apply 後直接進 Health Connect 與後續 ack，無法確認快照已落盤；改為檢查雙份 commit，失敗拋出後由上游捕捉、保留 DataItem，不把保存失敗當訊號拒收刪除。取消仍傳播，移除重複 timestamp_source 寫入。手機全部單元測試、Debug／Release 通過（heart-rate-snapshot-durability-20261005.log）。實機磁碟故障／重送尚未注入，不稱已驗收全部同步成功；正式加密仍未切換。

儲存失敗重試稽核：control-status 即使排序判為 no-op，仍透過 status／raw durability checkpoint 的 commit 成功才 ack，未確認此路徑有誤刪資料。加密偏好隔離測試追加「寫入失敗但記憶體已更新→未持久化值仍是前值→空 editor commit 重試完整快照」驗證 PASS（raw-failure-retry-device-20261005.json）；手機 Debug 建置／安裝成功。這只驗證明確同步重試，不代表所有 apply 失敗均會自動補寫；正式切換仍須逐呼叫端保留失敗訊息及避免錯誤 ack。

加密偏好冷啟動驗收：雙端單元測試、Release 及手機 Debug 通過（raw-registry-restart-final-20261005.log）；手機更新 Success。隔離 registry 將 apply／commit 寫入加密檔後確認 prepare PASS，以 am force-stop 終止，再冷啟動 verify mode，重新載入 KeyStore 包裝金鑰與已提交 marker，讀回 count／文字、clear 寫入空加密快照與 fixture 清理 PASS（raw-registry-restart-device-20261005.json）。未操作正式資料、未量測腕部訊號；正式切換還需 apply 拒收／持久化失敗對各呼叫端的处理及 UI 存取錯誤呈現。

清空通知契約修正：本 App target36 在 Android R+ 清空時發送 null key，全部 listener 通知在 main thread；不把 clear 刪除的所有鍵冒充逐鍵變更。依 Android 官方 SharedPreferences.OnSharedPreferenceChangeListener 文件核對。手機 Debug 建置／安裝成功，raw_preferences mode 新增 null key／main Looper 斷言後 PASS（raw-clear-notification-device-20261005.json）。這驗證本機 API36，不代表 API28／29 行為全部驗收；正式 RawSensorStore 仍未切換。

現有正式快照相容稽核：手機更新成功，audit_raw 使用實際 raw_sensor_results 的固定讀取快照，1687 個鍵、序列化 232677 bytes；隔離加密副本寫入、重新解密與逐位元 codec 核對 PASS，副本清理完成。證據 raw-production-audit-20261005.json；未修改來源、未上傳波形，也未啟用正式加密切換。此驗證不代表最新量測或 UI 全驗收；手錶 ADB 當時 offline。

加密偏好背壓修正：原單執行緒 executor 的無界佇列改為最多 4 個等待工作；超界拒收，不修改未被接受的記憶體快照，commit 回 false 並記錄拒收型別。手機 Debug 建置／安裝成功；隔離 raw_backpressure mode 將首筆寫入阻塞、填滿佇列、確認第 6 筆 commit 失敗且不顯示該值，放行後確認順序與後續重試 PASS（raw-backpressure-device-20261005.json）。apply 超界仍拋 RejectedExecutionException，正式呼叫端需明確處理，不能靜默掉資料；正式 RawSensorStore 未切換。

加密偏好串接與單實例驗收：EncryptedRawPreferencesRegistry 序列化開啟／遷移，同一路徑與名稱共用 EncryptedSnapshotPreferences，避免不同入口各自寫入快照。手機 Debug 建置／安裝成功；隔離 raw_registry mode 驗證同實例、apply 更新立即跨入口可見、commit 後直接解密讀回、clear 持久化及舊明文不存在 PASS（raw-registry-device-20261005.json）。正式 RawSensorStore 尚未切換；完整通知相容、佇列界限、重啟 registry 與正式資料讀回仍待驗收。

SharedPreferences 相容介面已加入 EncryptedSnapshotPreferences：apply 立即更新記憶體後進單執行緒寫入，commit 等待該順序中的寫入結果；寫入失敗回 false／記錄例外型別，不冒充持久化成功。主執行緒 listener 通知、型別讀取、集合隔離及 Editor 已接上。手機 Debug 建置、安裝通過，隔離 raw_preferences mode 驗證阻塞磁碟時 apply 可讀、apply→commit 持久化順序、通知、失敗回報、clear 與集合隔離 PASS（raw-preferences-device-20261005.json）。尚未測完整 Android clear(null) 通知相容性、程序重啟／加密檔實際串接、singleton／遷移鎖与多畫面驗收；正式 RawSensorStore 未切換。本批未做 Release 全回歸。

Editor 變更集已實作：每鍵最後 put/remove 勝出、null 作 remove、clear 在持久資料合併前執行而不丟同一 editor 待提交值、drain 後可重用、集合深複製。4 項新回歸與 raw snapshot 定向測試通過（raw-snapshot-editor-20261005.log）。目前仍是相容層內部元件；SharedPreferences 公開介面、apply 非同步與 commit 確認順序、listener 通知及實機故障驗收未完成，正式儲存不切換。本批未安裝。

原始快照更新核心：RawSnapshotTransactions 在同一實例鎖內從持久資料讀取、合併／clear／remove、寫入再逐位元讀回核對；失敗不回成功，也不以舊記憶體快照掩蓋已寫入但確認失敗的狀態。深複製字串集合，避免外部修改已保存證據。新增 5 項測試，包含 20 個並行獨立鍵更新，與全部 raw snapshot 定向測試通過（raw-snapshot-transactions-20261005.log）。正式 SharedPreferences adapter、單實例共享與 apply 順序仍待接入；本批未安裝、不宣稱正式儲存已切換。

遷移競態稽核：提交前重新序列化來源，若複製期間已有更新則拒絕提交／清理，保留新來源與未提交目標。新增來源變更回歸，7 項遷移測試通過（raw-migration-race-20261005.log）。這不是完整並行安全：最後核對到 marker 提交之間仍須由正式單一存取層持有寫入鎖，Editor／apply 的順序與磁碟提交仍待實作驗證；不得因此啟用正式遷移。本批尚未安裝。

雙端安裝確認：手機最新 Debug 安裝 Success；手錶新連線確認 Xiaomi Watch 2 Pro，Wear Debug 覆蓋安裝 Success，package 回讀 3.13.37／147。手錶當時電量 3%、USB 充電，未啟動腕部掃描，不把安裝當感測驗收。

Android 原始快照遷移 adapter：KeyStore 包裝獨立隨機金鑰、noBackup 加密檔、明確版本提交標記、逐位元讀回核對後才清理原 SharedPreferences。手機完整單元測試、Debug／Release 通過（raw-migration-android-20261005.log）；覆蓋安裝成功，隨機隔離偏好資料遷移、重新開啟與明文清理實機 PASS（raw-migration-android-device-20261005.json），未動正式 raw_sensor_results。仍須完成 SharedPreferences 相容 Editor／並行與 commit/apply 語意驗證後才能正式切換。

原始快照遷移協調器：新增 RawSnapshotMigration，先逐位元核對序列化來源／目標，再持久化提交標記與讀回確認，最後才清理舊資料；已提交但目標遺失／無法解密時不重建空庫，目標衝突不覆寫，清理失敗可追蹤重試。6 項遷移測試與既有快照測試通過（raw-snapshot-migration-20261005.log）。仍未接入正式 Android SharedPreferences／marker adapter，不宣稱正式快照已加密。

加密快照真正進程中斷驗收：手機新版 Debug 安裝 Success；隔離 fixture 完成正常加密檔後，開始下一次 AtomicFile 寫入、寫入部分內容並 fsync，回報 partial_write_waiting_for_force_stop，確認 App PID 存在後以 am force-stop 終止。冷啟動 verify_raw_interrupt 重載 KeyStore 包裝金鑰、讀回前份快照、重新寫入讀回與隔離資料清理 PASS，證據 raw-snapshot-process-interrupt-20261005.json。這是 App 進程終止，不是斷電／磁碟滿測試；正式遷移仍未啟用。最新 ADB 僅手機在線，未進行手錶腕部採集。

加密快照檔驗證：RawSnapshotCipher 使用隨機 nonce 的 AES-GCM，格式版本與用途為 AAD；EncryptedRawSnapshotFile 原子寫入、fsync、完整讀回及解密驗證。手機／Wear 單元測試、手機 Debug 與雙端 Release 建置通過（raw-snapshot-file-final-20261005.log）。手機 Debug 更新成功，隔離 fixture 的加密讀回、寫入失敗回復、錯誤金鑰拒收及 fixture 刪除實機 PASS（raw-snapshot-file-device-20261005.json）。非強制終止／斷電測試；未啟用正式原始快照遷移，不代表 SharedPreferences 已加密。Hermes 本機審查提出空間不足／重啟／回復邊界，尚待故障注入；Grokbot 仍未驗證接口。

原始快照加密前置：17 個 Kotlin 消費者及刪除流程已统一經 RawSensorStore，保留既有資料檔案；手機單元測試、Debug／Release 通過。新增 RawSnapshotCodec，保留所有 SharedPreferences 支援型別與 float 原始位元，拒絕截斷、尾隨資料、錯誤 Unicode、不支援型別與超界輸入；6 項定向測試通過（raw-snapshot-codec-20261005.log）。此 codec 尚未接入正式存取，原始快照仍未加密，不宣稱完成遷移。後續須實作原子加密寫入、斷電恢復、讀回核對與金鑰遺失保護後才能刪舊檔。

## 2026-10-05 encryption implementation gate

- Phone forced-termination test passed at an actual copy transaction checkpoint: PID disappeared, cold restart used a different PID, source evidence remained intact, partial destination rolled back, retry copied and verified all rows. Evidence: `evidence-interruption-checkpoint-20261005.json`, `evidence-interruption-verify-20261005.json`. Production cutover/legacy-file cleanup are the remaining encryption gates.

- Cross-process phone test passed: isolated encrypted evidence/key fixture prepared, force-stop verified no surviving PID, cold start used a different PID, matching fixture read back exact raw/derived data and cleanup passed. Evidence: `evidence-restart-prepare-20261005.json`, `evidence-restart-verify-20261005.json`. This closes isolated encrypted process-reopen only; production migration/cutover remain pending.

- New hardware evidence: Debug-only isolated storage validation ran on the phone and both database/key groups returned PASS (`evidence-storage-device-20261005.json`). Covers encrypted copy/reopen/wrong-key, transactional copy rollback, derived-only/complete deletion, key corruption/loss and source preservation. UUID test artifacts were cleaned; no production evidence migrated. Release manifest excludes the DUMP-protected debug Activity.

- Added isolated Keystore corruption/loss/reload tests and readback verification of the persisted passphrase envelope. Production encryption remains disabled. Runtime test environment has a live software-emulator process but an offline ADB transport; no test execution is claimed.

- Added source-preserving transactional evidence copy with exact row/provenance verification, bounded session enumeration and rejection of populated/same-file destinations. Added encrypted copy/reopen/failure rollback tests; production migration remains disabled pending device execution and crash-recovery cutover.

- Added SQLCipher Room builder, Keystore-wrapped random passphrase storage and encrypted reopen/wrong-key/raw-text instrumentation coverage. See `evidence-encryption-2026-10-05.md`.
- Production Room initialization is deliberately not switched before source-preserving migration and device tests pass; existing phone evidence remains unchanged. Encryption implementation is not yet release acceptance.

## 2026-10-05 evidence deletion gate

- Hermes local model review returned advisory deletion acceptance suggestions (`D:/AI/AI/04 代理交換/responses/20261005-xhb-hermes-deletion-review.md`). It supplied no hardware result and did not identify a proven transaction defect.
- Added injected SQL deletion-failure coverage: deletion abort must preserve session/raw/derived tables, retry must succeed, and new sessions must remain writable. Instrumentation execution is still pending.
- Preference-store exceptions now count as individual deletion failures rather than aborting all remaining cleanup. Coroutine cancellation is preserved.
- Build verification: instrumentation APK compilation passed in `deletion-rollback-20261005.log`; final mobile unit tests and Debug/Release assemblies passed in `deletion-cancellation-20261005.log`. Instrumentation tests have not run; watch remains charging in the latest preflight.

- Follow-up confirmed a user-facing defect: privacy clear omitted the Room evidence archive. It now calls transactional archive deletion and performs storage work on an IO dispatcher; confirmation names raw evidence/derived results and explains that future measurements can create new data.
- Follow-up verification: mobile unit tests, both Debug APK assemblies and Release assembly passed (`privacy-evidence-final-20261005.log`); main Debug APK installation returned Success. Actual deletion of user data was not performed; instrumentation assertions remain unverified.
- Added clear-all idempotence and foreign-key-order instrumentation coverage. No real user evidence was deleted during development. Device execution remains blocked by test-package installation policy; application-level archive encryption remains open.

- Fresh instrumentation installation again returned `INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED: Install canceled by user`; no database instrumentation tests executed. Main-app installation success does not establish permission to install the separate test package.
- Added explicit transactional session deletion in SignalEvidenceDao, removing derived results, raw samples and session in foreign-key order. Ordinary derived-only deletion remains separate.
- Added file-backed reopen coverage for selected-session deletion, repeated deletion and preservation of another session. This test remains pending device execution; compilation is not deletion acceptance.
- Code audit confirms SignalEvidenceDatabase currently uses the standard Room builder without an application-level encryption factory. Wear outbox AES-GCM encryption does not prove this archive is encrypted. Archive encryption remains an open release requirement.
- Verification: mobile unit tests, Debug main/test APK and Release assembly passed (`.tooling/verify/evidence-delete-20261005.log`). The new deletion API is not yet connected to a user-facing deletion control; instrumented deletion and reopen assertions remain unexecuted.

## Current source of truth — 2026-10-03

- **Hermes follow-up:** local Hermes reviewed the sanitized sleep fallback. Its integer-overflow concern does not apply to the current `Double` duration sum, so it was not adopted as a defect; the useful boundary-test principle is retained for finite/large interval totals. Grokbot still has no verified API endpoint.

- **AI integration audit:** local Ollama is reachable on `127.0.0.1:11434` and exposes `hermes3:8b` plus other local models; the sanitized Hermes archive review is therefore reproducible locally. A separate local HTTP listener exists on port 8080, but its API identity/schema is not verified as Grokbot, so it is not invoked or treated as Grok evidence. No health data or source was sent externally.

- **Sleep UI acceptance:** opening the Health tab and waiting for its foreground query now shows `22.8 小時`; diagnostics confirm `sleep_session_fallback_used=true`, 14 complete intervals, no read failures, and platform aggregate unavailable. This is a seven-day merged session duration, not today's sleep and not sleep-stage/medical sleep time; the UI detail label carries that limitation. Hermes local review has been incorporated for archive failure cases; Grokbot remains unverified because no endpoint/model is configured.

- **Sleep overlap safety:** the session fallback now merges overlapping complete intervals before summing, preventing duplicate-source or nested records from inflating duration. It labels the result as a merged session interval estimate and preserves the platform-aggregate-unavailable state. Unit tests and Debug assembly passed; the latest Debug install returned Success. A fresh diagnostic timestamp was not observed in the final run, so new UI readback is not claimed.

- **Sleep fallback:** when Health Connect's platform sleep aggregate is unavailable but complete non-overlapping SleepSession intervals are readable, the summary now sums those intervals and labels the value as a non-platform session sum. The original aggregate status remains visible in diagnostics; no sleep-stage duration is claimed. Mobile unit tests passed, Debug APK installed, and the device readback still shows 14 complete intervals with no read failures.

- **Health Connect missing-value verification:** after reinstall and foreground refresh, diagnostics now show `oxygen_today_records=0` and `oxygen_read_state=本次查詢期間無紀錄`; this is a confirmed empty today query, not a permission failure. Sleep returned 14 complete history intervals, while the platform aggregate is unavailable; the app now preserves that distinction instead of showing generic read failure. Per-metric exception details are persisted when a query actually fails. Debug/unit validation passed and the new mobile Debug APK installed successfully. This does not prove watch-origin data exists or that platform aggregate semantics are clinically suitable.

- **Missing-value diagnosis:** permission-list failure now clears all 12 Health Connect cards, not just four, preventing stale heart-rate/oxygen/environment values from surviving a failed refresh. Oxygen shows its actual read-state rather than generic waiting text. Added bounded local query diagnostics without physiological values. Mobile unit/Debug/Release validation passed and installed successfully. Device readback reports oxygen and sleep `READ_FAILED`, not proven absent records; zero counts here must not be interpreted as successful empty queries. Added detailed failure-category logging for the next build; exact failure classification is still pending.

- **Restart recovery isolation:** MainActivity now uses the existing per-item dispatch policy for retained DataItems, so malformed items do not terminate all subsequent recovery. Cancellation propagates instead of being classified as invalid data; DataItem buffer release remains in finally. Three dispatch regressions and Debug assembly passed (`pending-recovery-isolation.log`). This follow-up is not installed and does not establish automatic archive retry scheduling or successful device restart readback.

- **PPG archive acknowledgment:** accepted final snapshots and completed-receipt replays now store/verify the Room preview before deleting the DataItem. ## Expanded user objectives — 2026-10-03

The following supplement the existing goal; they do not replace acquisition/sync/clinical integrity acceptance gates.

1. **Functional coverage:** compare Apple Watch, Samsung Galaxy Watch and Google Pixel Watch against this actual device's public APIs, hardware evidence and permissions. Maintain a per-function matrix: supported, implemented, hardware-verified, unavailable with reason. Do not infer OEM feature access from a sensor/chip inventory entry alone. Verify competitor claims against current primary documentation before assigning parity targets.
2. **Complete user experience:** health UI must expose all supported measured metrics, explain missing/stale/rejected/unmapped/uncalibrated states and provide actionable measurement/sync paths. No fake zeroes or synthetic measurements to hide failures. Manual entries (weight/hydration), historical values and model estimates must retain distinct labels and timestamps.
3. **AILISA Full Coverage:** preserve immutable raw evidence plus a separately regenerable finite analysis sequence. Complete provenance, quality-based strategy, actual linear/spline/Kalman implementations, validated numerical-model fallback, Room derived storage, PPG integration and origin/confidence overlays. Unknown sample clocks use index-domain only; unmapped multi-channel payloads are not presumed a single time series. Completeness cannot enable HRV, physiological conversion or diagnosis without independent evidence.
4. **AI questioning:** retain local deterministic analysis; add Gemini and a third provider (initially ChatGPT) on phone/watch. Distinguish opening installed assistant/web UI from embedded API inference. User must initiate external sharing; entry buttons do not auto-upload health data. Actual assistant availability, account/network requirements and missing-browser fallback require hardware acceptance. Gemma explanation runtime remains a separate incomplete integration.
5. **Delivery gate:** paired builds/tests, code review and three UI-started scans with acquisition→sync→persistent readback→freshness→UI evidence, then signing/release checks. Unavailable OEM mappings/calibration remain explicit unresolved items; do not promise competitor parity or medical accuracy before evidence.

Watch prefers the installed com.google.android.wearable.assistant launcher; its actual availability/identity is not yet verified. No prompt, value or waveform is attached to launch intents. These are external questioning entry points, not API clients. Build batch is recorded in .tooling/verify/external-ai-entry-build.log; install and real assistant response remain unverified for this patch.

- **Health Connect cumulative-activity aggregation audit (2026-09-29):** official guidance says to use Aggregate APIs for cumulative steps and notes Activity/Sleep deduplication follows user source priority. Confirmed `MetricDetailActivity` step history and daily summary use `aggregate` / `aggregateGroupByPeriod`; ambiguous raw interval handling remains limited to metrics built from raw records (including recorded-total-calorie diagnostics), where summing overlapping/multiple sources would be unsafe. No blanket removal of overlap gates is warranted. Provider behavior and source attribution still require live-device verification. Reference: [Health Connect aggregate data](https://developer.android.com/health-and-fitness/health-connect/aggregate-data), [Health Connect raw data guidance](https://developer.android.com/health-and-fitness/health-connect/read-data).
- **Sensor HAL raw-event audit (2026-09-29):** the screenshot's `hal_event_values_unscaled` / 2170-point ECG event dump confirms receipt of raw event fields, not decoded ECG samples or calibrated physiological values. Android defines `SensorEvent.values` layout by sensor type; vendor-specific temporary types may be undocumented. The Wear capture deliberately preserves ECG raw rows and labels them unmapped; the mobile ECG policy rejects waveform interpretation unless an explicitly verified encoding is supplied. Repeated values such as `48438` cannot be identified as samples, sentinels, or packed fields without OEM mapping; no unit conversion is justified. Reviewed `RawVitalsProbe`, `WearRawSensorPublisher`, and phone export policy; no device is connected, so this is source/screenshot analysis only. Primary references: Android [Sensors](https://source.android.com/docs/core/interaction/sensors), [SensorEvent](https://developer.android.com/reference/android/hardware/SensorEvent), [Sensors HAL](https://source.android.com/docs/core/interaction/sensors/sensors-hal2).
- **Wear listener fault isolation (2026-09-29):** `AdvancedSensorController` and `AmbientLightController` now isolate per-sensor registration failures and listener-cleanup `RuntimeException`s so one failing operation does not abort other sensors/cleanup. Wear Debug tests 167/167 and `:wear:assembleRelease` / `:wear:lintRelease` pass; unsigned Wear artifact SHA-256 updated above. No connected device; battery behavior and runtime sensor acquisition remain unverified.
- **Raw sensor wakeup fallback exception audit (2026-09-29):** found `RawVitalsProbe.switchToWakeupIfNoSamples()` directly unregistered the active sensor and registered the wakeup fallback on a Handler callback; a `RuntimeException` could escape, while registration failure left the terminal reason generic. Cleanup failure is now isolated and prevents an unsafe switch; it stops processing callbacks (`running=false`) but retains the last sensor reference for later cleanup, without asserting that Android successfully unregistered the OS listener. Fallback permission/registration failures are caught and retained as explicit terminal reasons. Regression coverage proves no fallback register after cleanup failure; existing tests also cover false/security/runtime/success registration outcomes. Cursor Agent main reviewer plus three role subagents reviewed only the supplied code summary (not repository/device); they agreed callback/OS state must not be inferred and noted tests do not exercise actual SensorManager behavior. Static call-site review found probe fallback and service stop use main-looper handlers; no cross-thread race was demonstrated in those paths. Wear Debug 169/169; `:wear:assembleRelease` and `:wear:lintRelease` pass. Updated unsigned Wear APK hash above. ADB still has no connected devices; listener cleanup and fallback remain unverified on hardware.

- **Sensor registration failure cause preservation (2026-09-29):** code review found `RawVitalsProbe.start()` overwrote `permission_denied` / `registration_exception` set by its failure callback with generic `registration_failed`, and the failure callback duplicated start/stop diagnostic writes. Preserve specific causes, use the generic fallback only for unclassified false registration, and centralize the diagnostic write in `start()`. Regression test added; full Mobile 536/536 and Wear 164/164, both Release assemblies and lint pass. It now retries fallback as before, but rethrows the primary exception only when fallback also fails; the outer guard records `registration_exception`. Added regression coverage and kept UI descriptions consistent. Both app test suites, Release assemblies, and Release lint pass; APKs are unsigned and no device scan was performed.

- **HAL sample-rate audit:** biomedical waveform rate is derived only from valid `SensorAdditionalInfo.TYPE_SAMPLING`; missing metadata yields 0 rather than event-throughput inference. Targeted `OpticalSignalQualityTest` covers this policy. HAL period remains a reported sampling period, not device clinical calibration.
- **HAL / algorithm integration decision (2026-09-29):** do not treat absent clinical calibration as a reason to leave all hardware unused. For each endpoint, track exposed → permission/registration → callback → payload decoded (schema/unit/timebase) → eligible algorithm/output → persisted → rendered. Implement useful standard-sensor algorithms and research estimates whenever their own documented inputs and assumptions pass; a missing input blocks only dependent outputs. Keep raw biomedical capture, quality analysis, nonclinical estimates, and clinical diagnosis as separate capabilities. Current chip catalogs are capability hypotheses/feature mappings, not proof every UI route is wired or a sensor is usable. The ECG screenshot's 2,170 raw values establish event-value receipt only; the repeated `48438` cannot be assigned meaning without the OEM event schema. Next code-review deliverable: produce a per-endpoint state table from current registration/consumer code and tests, identifying genuinely unconnected app paths separately from OEM-gated, no-event, raw-only, and already-working features. No device scan or code behavior change is claimed by this documentation update.

Added `WearSensorSuiteTimingTest.everySensorKindIsQueuedOrExplicitlyDocumentedAsAnAlias` so every enum kind must belong to a production scan queue or an explicit alias set, and queue categories cannot overlap. `:wear:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.jaysato.xiaomihealthbridge.WearSensorSuiteTimingTest` passes. This guards scan-plan coverage only; it does not establish live sensor/API availability or device measurements.

## Unified project checkpoint — 2026-09-29

- **Objective:** prioritize trustworthy end-to-end health-data delivery over adding more algorithms: watch acquisition → payload/provenance → quality/calibration → Data Layer → durable phone storage → freshness → UI. Count every started scan, including cancellation, timeout and partial results; do not target or claim 100% success.
- **Short term:** classify each stage outcome and preserve event-time/source/units; separate no sensor event, permission/registration failure, timeout, quality rejection, uncalibrated raw data, transport failure, persistence failure, stale data and UI suppression.
- **Acceptance by 10/4:** three non-overlapping, UI-started paired watch/phone scans while worn and unplugged. For each, reconcile the same session through watch capture, Data Layer receipt, phone persistence, process-restart readback and displayed state. Record failures as outcomes; do not substitute old snapshots for fresh measurements.
- **Long term:** fault-replay tests, encrypted-export lifecycle verification, reference-instrument paired research under appropriate consent/review, then signing and release checks. No clinical/diagnostic claim without device-specific evidence and applicable validation.
- **HAL ruling:** Android's Sensors HAL feeds the Android sensor framework; `SensorManager` exposes sensor descriptors/events, not a general app interface to chip registers. Enumeration is not proof of activation or data. Standard Android sensor types can use documented units; vendor ECG/PPG/BioZ payloads remain raw unless their schema, timebase, units and device calibration are established. The screenshot's `2170` is not evidence of 2170 ECG beats or calibrated waveform samples. See the dated HAL audit below and `docs/watch-hardware-capability-matrix.md`.
- **HAL inventory triage (2026-09-29):** consolidated exposed endpoints into (1) physical motion/environment sensors (accelerometer, gyro, magnetic field, pressure, ambient light), usable for bounded engineering features after timestamp/source checks; (2) Android fused/logical endpoints (rotation vector, gravity, linear acceleration, motion states), useful as derived features but not extra chips; (3) duplicate delivery/representation variants (wake-up/non-wakeup, calibrated/uncalibrated, step detector/counter), which must be deduplicated or explicitly chosen; and (4) vendor biomedical endpoints (HR/PPG/SpO₂/ECG/BioZ/temperature/ADT), which may provide raw events but lack per-device interpretation evidence in this project. For each endpoint, acceptance must separately record enumerated → permission/registration → callback → payload schema/unit → timestamp/freshness → snapshot → phone receipt/persistence → UI; label the exact first failing stage instead of calling a sensor simply “unused.” The current screenshot’s ECG row count/value dump is an unmapped `SensorEvent.values` preview; repeated numbers do not establish sample meaning. This is a static source/document audit only: SDK ADB reports no connected device, so no new watch scan was possible.
- **Current gate:** unit tests/builds are software evidence only. Paired-device acceptance, latest-install verification, restart readback and visual confirmation remain outstanding whenever ADB is unavailable; Release delivery additionally requires the existing signing key. Never claim scan success, installation, clinical accuracy or release readiness without fresh evidence.

## Latest progress — 2026-09-29

- **Phone persistence and heart-rate retry follow-up (2026-09-29):** `/control-status/` now commits both status stores before ACK, with unique retry checkpoints; failures retain the DataItem. Workout-route and sync-state persistence also use checked commits on `Dispatchers.IO`, retaining the source item on failure. Heart-rate queue writes require successful `commit()` before ACK; when the 2,000-minute queue is full, the writer flushes an existing batch before appending rather than silently truncating older samples. Health Connect heart-rate writes use one-minute records with a stable per-minute `clientRecordId` and measurement-time `clientRecordVersion`; a Fake Health Connect integration test verifies identical replay deduplicates and a newer same-minute sample updates the record. Added route-store and queue-capacity policy regressions. Official semantics: [Health Connect Metadata](https://developer.android.com/reference/androidx/health/connect/client/records/metadata/Metadata). Full Mobile 502/502 and Wear 156/156 pass; Mobile Release assembly/lint pass; Wear Release assembly/lint passed in the prior validation. Unsigned Mobile Release SHA-256: `CDF92B7EFDA43841DF5FEB43E5A764121FAE3A3E99381D79CA87B9EC646EEE14`; Wear Release remains `AE291186DC26B9A1428C1B874538F67317A0EBE40348DAD6D9F934D309679CB2`. SDK `adb` runs, but its current device list is empty; no install, paired scan, restart readback, or visual hardware acceptance. Original release signing identity remains unavailable.
- **Safety-alert durable receipt (2026-09-29):** source audit found `/safety-alert/` persisted deduplication/event state with asynchronous `apply()` and then ACKed the DataItem; this created a code-visible crash window, not a confirmed historical loss. Alert state now commits atomically before ACK; commit failure retains the item and marks the same receipt for retry. Notification/cancel side effects are best-effort after durable event recording and failures are logged, so this does not guarantee OS notification delivery. Reused the tested commit-before-ack gate. Provenance explicitly says platform Activity aggregate and source count; no cross-source manual sum was introduced. Replaced the obsolete single-origin calorie-aggregate policy/test with `acceptsDeduplicatedActivityTotal`, whose contract explicitly permits a valid multi-origin platform total. This avoids an endless background retry loop without evicting or silently deleting the snapshot. - Wear final-snapshot delivery hardening: encrypted outbox entries no longer schedule background work when the immediate Data Layer send succeeds; direct-send failure schedules an `APPEND_OR_REPLACE` retry, and Wear UI/service startup reschedules orphaned durable entries (`KEEP` prevents duplicate startup recovery chains). The old persisted `worker_enqueued` gate was removed to avoid a stale flag suppressing retry scheduling. Workers append a bounded continuation when work remains after a 12-record batch. Pending snapshots are ordered by path timestamp, producer sequence, then stable ID rather than random encrypted filename; regression coverage added. Crash-left `.tmp` files are now authenticated/decrypted and restored to the pending queue when complete, otherwise quarantined and capacity-accounted. Queue-full UI copy no longer implies direct delivery succeeded. The same stable DataItem path is retained for idempotent replacement; Data Layer task success still means platform acceptance, not phone persistence.

Confirmed residual risks: process death after durable write but before callback scheduling strands data until the next Activity/Service recovery; persistent send failures retry with exponential backoff without a terminal attempt cap; quarantined files have no user-facing cleanup and count against capacity; `.tmp` recovery is implemented but lacks device/instrumentation testing; if encryption/queueing fails and direct send also fails, there is no durable copy. Outbox tests still lack Android Keystore roundtrip/tamper, WorkManager restart/retry/cancel, callback-worker race, >12-item replay and phone durable-ack integration tests. ## Sensor HAL / unused-channel audit — 2026-09-29

- **Definition:** Android Sensors HAL is the OEM implementation between sensor hardware/drivers and Android's sensor framework. This app reads public framework events through `SensorManager`; a HAL sensor listing is not direct chip-register access, proof of sensor activity, or proof that a physiological value is available. The capability matrix consolidates 66 framework endpoints, not 66 physical chips; wake/non-wake variants and virtual/fused sensors must not be counted as separate physical hardware.
- **Code evidence:** Wear `RawVitalsProbe` enumerates public sensor types and the suite processes `Kind.entries` in a bounded sequence. `WearRawSensorPublisher` transports ECG as `hal_event_values_unscaled` and multi-channel values as `hal_event_values_unmapped`. Current code supports selected motion/orientation, pressure, light, compass and step use cases; biomedical pathways may preserve raw observations for diagnostics without deriving calibrated physiology. See `wear/src/main/java/com/jaysato/xiaomihealthbridge/RawVitalsProbe.kt`, `WearControlService.kt`, `WearRawSensorPublisher.kt`, and `docs/watch-hardware-capability-matrix.md`.
- **Per-family source audit (code inspection only; not a live device scan):**

  | Sensor family / endpoints | App path found | Why a health number may be absent / classification |
  |---|---|---|
  | Android standard motion: accelerometer, gyro, gravity, linear acceleration, rotation/orientation, magnetometer | `RawVitalsProbe.Kind` includes public `android.sensor.*` types; `WearControlService` also has dedicated listeners for selected motion/orientation sensors; `CompassActivity` uses rotation vector | These are active framework APIs, but duplicate calibrated/uncalibrated and fused endpoints are not independent features. Motion samples support context/navigation candidates, not a diagnosis. |
  | Pressure and ambient light | Standard `android.sensor.pressure` / `android.sensor.light` acquisition; service computes relative altitude and records lux for its feature paths | Pressure-to-altitude depends on a reference pressure and environmental conditions; lux is environmental context. Neither is a clinical vital sign. |
  | Step counter / step detector and motion triggers | Step counter is sampled; step detector, significant-motion, stationary/motion, wrist-tilt are separately classified as motion-trigger kinds | Trigger endpoints may emit only after movement and one-shot sensors need explicit trigger behavior; zero callbacks in a still/short scan is not proof of broken hardware. Step totals also need source/time-window deduplication against Health Services/Health Connect. |
  | Heart-rate / LifeQ logical endpoints | Wear Health Services and raw public endpoints are separate code paths; raw probe includes `HEART_RATE`, `MAX_HEART_RATE`, `LIFE_Q_OTT` | A catalog entry is not a measurement. Only an explicit, fresh, correctly typed BPM result can be displayed as HR; LifeQ/optical availability alone does not establish one. |
  | PPG, raw SpO₂, ECG, BioZ | Suite queues bounded raw acquisition; publisher transports events and metadata. ECG payload is explicitly unmapped; BioZ respiration is an alias of the same BioZ endpoint rather than a second acquisition | OEM mode/contact/permission and event schema can gate callbacks. Received integers are not automatically waveform samples or calibrated bpm, %, mV, Ω, RR, or HRV. The screenshot's repeated `48438` remains uninterpreted. |
  | Xiaomi SpO₂ algorithm (`mi_spo2`), ADT/off-body, body/board temperature, thermometer | Vendor endpoints are represented in the catalog/probe; some require OEM mode or contact | These are proprietary/conditional endpoints. The code path can report registration/no-event/raw state, but current repository evidence does not establish their output units, clinical meaning, or that this watch exposes a usable result. |

  This audit finds several capabilities are **not wholly unused**: standard motion/environment channels have active app consumers, and biomedical endpoints have bounded acquisition and raw transport. The remaining gap is often interpretation/verification or a gated/no-event OEM route, not absence of all code. The source-only inspection cannot establish which endpoint is emitting on the current watch. The matrix's chip-family labels remain tentative where the BOM is not confirmed.
- **UI terminology cross-check:** the supplied screenshot says “HAL 原始事件（未處理）”, but current source labels the detail as “Sensor API 未解碼事件值” and states that saved rows are Android callback `FloatArray` values, not a binary HAL packet or decoded/calibrated ECG (`mobile/src/main/java/com/jaysato/xiaomihealthbridge/RawSensorDetailActivity.kt`). The screenshot therefore does not match the current source wording (possibly an older installed build or another page); no live UI/build identity is available to establish which. Internal wire encoding names are historical and were not renamed without migration review.
- **Screenshot interpretation:** the 2026-09-28 image shows 2,170 ECG-typed raw values, but explicitly says event time/encoding is unknown and does not calculate RR/HR/HRV. Repeated values such as `48438` cannot be identified as samples, sentinels, or packet fields without Xiaomi's event-format contract; do not interpret them. This is raw-event receipt, not proof of a decoded ECG waveform or a missing sensor.
- **Primary-source cross-check:** AOSP defines HAL as the interface the OEM implements for Android's sensor framework; Android recommends Health Services for standard Wear OS activity/health metrics in most cases. MAX86178 documentation describes chip-level PPG/ECG/BioZ capabilities and reference/evaluation setups, not the Watch 2 Pro's electrode/optical configuration, event encoding, units, sample clock, gain, or calibration. References: https://source.android.com/docs/core/interaction/sensors/sensor-stack ; https://source.android.com/docs/core/interaction/sensors/sensors-hal2 ; https://developer.android.com/health-and-fitness/health-services ; https://www.analog.com/en/products/max86178.html .
- **Decision / acceptance:** classify every endpoint independently as exposed → selected/registered → callback/event received → payload decoded with verified unit/timebase → eligible for a named feature/formula → persisted and rendered. Distinguish duplicates/virtual sensors, trigger/OEM-gated channels, no-event channels, raw-only channels, and supported features. Do not call an endpoint "unused" solely because it has no clinical number; equally, do not call it functional solely because it is listed or emitted raw integers. The next HAL-specific code review must verify ECG event field/sample boundaries and timestamp provenance before labeling the payload a waveform or enabling any RR/HR computation.
- **Live-evidence limit:** the supplied screenshot is historical, and this audit environment has no `adb` executable; no current watch scan, HAL inventory refresh, or device behavior is verified in this entry. Device acceptance remains open.

## Meeting outcome


Six available internal reviewers contributed from PM, Android, Wear runtime, data pipeline, privacy, and QA perspectives. Codex is the decision owner and reconciles proposals against project policy and source evidence; review count is not proof of correctness. No external web AI was contacted and no health exports, identifiers, or source were sent externally.

**Decision:** stop widening feature scope. The next delivery is a bounded data-integrity and diagnosability release: establish traceable inputs and outcomes, prevent ambiguous aggregation, make watch-scan timeout/cleanup provable, and protect raw diagnostics. Then run one fresh paired-device acceptance session. Current/fresh values remain gated until metric-specific freshness rules are verified and implemented.

## Integrated status / single source of truth — 2026-09-29

- **Goal through 10/4:** evidence-first audits and fixes across phone + Wear; three non-overlapping UI-started paired scans with every initiated run counted (complete/partial/cancelled/timeout/failure/unknown); then full code review, hardware acceptance, signing/release checks. Do not call this complete until all gates pass. After 10/4, continue privacy/encryption lifecycle, reference-instrument studies, and authorized signed distribution.
- **Algorithm policy:** algorithms are required product functionality, not prohibited by lack of clinical validation. Calculate each output when that output's formula-specific inputs, units, provenance, sample coverage and time window qualify; a missing input only withholds dependent outputs. Distinguish directly observed measurements, formula-based estimates/research metrics, composite scores and clinical diagnostic claims. Never describe an estimate as OEM-calibrated or diagnostic without device-specific evidence. This reconciles the user's algorithm request with the evidence gates; see “Algorithm policy re-review” below.
- **Data-flow acceptance:** sensor availability/registration → event payload and units → quality/calibration eligibility → watch snapshot → Data Layer order/receipt → phone durable storage → restart readback → freshness → UI. Retain separate measurement time and receipt time; classify missing, rejected, stale, hidden, permission-denied, query-failed, partial, and uncalibrated independently. Existing 30-minute display and 10-minute analysis windows are app policies, not clinical thresholds.
- **Latest code verification:** 441 mobile + 149 Wear Debug unit tests pass (0 failures/errors); focused snapshot ordering/retry tests pass; Debug and unsigned Release APK assemblies and both Release lint tasks pass. Current artifact hashes are recorded at the latest dated entry below. A previous mobile install was cancelled by MIUI (`INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED`); do not retry until the install confirmation is accepted. Release signing still requires the original key.
- **Current devices:** phone ADB online, installed mobile v146 / 3.13.36, USB-powered at 100%; watch ADB online, installed Wear v147 / 3.13.37, off-charge at 47%. No fresh paired scan has been run; watch-worn/UI state is unverified. Do not infer sensor success from online ADB or a successful build.
- **Next action:** after owner accepts the MIUI install prompt and both devices are ready (phone unlocked; watch worn, awake, off charger), install the current mobile Debug artifact without clearing data, launch the in-app engineering/debug UI, execute one paired scan, and reconcile the full pipeline before scheduling runs two and three. If a device precondition is absent, proceed with code-level integration tests and preserve that acceptance gate rather than fabricating completion.

## Algorithm availability review — 2026-09-29

Six internal Codex reviewer roles were asked to challenge the health-insight outputs; five returned before this record was written. No external AI or health export was used. All reviewers agreed that algorithms are appropriate and useful; the defect is treating missing inputs for one composite score as a reason to hide independent valid observations. Conversely, “other products use algorithms” is not evidence that this watch's raw channels have verified units, mappings, or clinical performance.

**Decision:** implement per-metric `available / partial / withheld` presentation. Show every independently valid observation with source, measurement time/window, and provenance. Compute an estimate only when that metric's own minimum-input contract is met; list used and missing inputs, coverage, and research/estimate label. Missing inputs are never imputed as normal or zero. Do not require a complete seven-day activity series to show independently measured HR/HRV observations; do not promote these into an absolute stress/readiness score until its formula and baseline assumptions are justified. Sleep session duration is not actual sleep or staging. Health Connect SpO₂ is displayed only within its actual observation window; no SpO₂ is inferred from uncalibrated raw PPG.

**Findings to fix/audit:** (1) The current ANS score requires same-day same-origin resting HR + HRV plus seven complete days of same-origin active-calorie data (`AlgorithmicInsightsEngine.kt` around 692–736), so a missing historical load suppresses the entire stress output. Separate measured components from composite scores and make dependencies visible. (2) A 96% SpO₂ card and “no SpO₂ in this analysis window” can both be correct if timestamps/windows differ; show observation time and queried window. The current query path already filters out-of-window records individually (`onlyInQueryWindow`) before summarization, so the suspected all-or-nothing loss is **not reproduced in current source**; retain the existing regression coverage and verify UI timestamps. (3) Health cards should show valid per-source observations even when source/interval ambiguity blocks aggregation; never sum overlapping sources. (4) ECG raw sample count with quality 0/100 may remain available for research, but RR/165.03 bpm must be marked rejected candidate, not a usable pulse or diagnosis; audit all UI consumers for accidental promotion.

**Acceptance tests:** partial HRV/RHR without seven-day load; missing RHR must not use ordinary HR 106; one SpO₂ record inside versus outside selected window; mixed-source/overlap data preserves per-source values but blocks aggregate; ECG quality 0, invalid clock, and out-of-range candidate preserve raw waveform while withholding physiological output. Track UI wording and dependencies separately from calculation output. This is product/data-integrity review, not clinical validation.

## Algorithm policy re-review — 2026-09-29

The user challenged the earlier interpretation that algorithms should be withheld. Six internal roles were asked to review the screenshots and algorithm policy; five returned (PM, Android/data integration, exercise physiology, QA, and research/privacy); one did not return. No external AI was contacted and no health export or device identifier was shared. Reviewers agreed: algorithms are appropriate and should be implemented; the problem is not that algorithms are inherently invalid. The current UI conflates availability of an individual metric with eligibility for a composite score, and some formulas currently in `PhysiologyAlgorithms.kt` are heuristics without a traceable validation source.

**Decision:** retain evidence-backed, reproducible algorithms and expose outputs at three levels: (1) directly measured observations with source/time; (2) formula-based descriptive or research estimates when that metric's own inputs are met; (3) composite/readiness or clinical outputs only when their complete, justified contracts are met. A missing composite input must never suppress an independently available result. Missing intervals are unknown, not zero. Inconsistent sources remain separately visible and are not summed. For each estimate, record formula/version, input list, units, coverage, and limitations.

**Code issues confirmed by review:**
- The ANS stress heuristic is gated on same-day RHR+RMSSD, same origin, profile age, and seven entire days of continuous active-calorie interval coverage. Sparse daily calorie records make the full-day coverage gate especially restrictive; this can suppress a score even when HR/HRV observations exist. Moreover, the hard-coded age-based “expected HRV”, 60 bpm reference and LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH calorie thresholds are not linked to a cited validated model in this implementation. Do not call this a literature-validated stress algorithm. Replace or relabel it before promoting a numeric stress score.
- `PhysiologyAlgorithms.sleep()` synthesizes deep/light/REM/awake proportions and score from duration, movement count and HRV. Actigraphy alone has limited/heterogeneous sleep-stage agreement with PSG; this heuristic must not be presented as measured sleep architecture or a reliable sleep-quality score. Prefer provider-reported stages when available; otherwise display session duration/observed movement only and label any experimental classifier explicitly.
- Banister TRIMP has a traceable established formula, but current implementation derives a per-session mean from sparse HR samples and relies on estimated maximum HR; identify this as rough relative load, report sample coverage, and do not silently imply calibrated individual training load. The traditional sex-specific coefficient cannot be computed when sex is unspecified; the current code correctly withholds it in that case.
- HR 106 bpm must not be substituted for resting HR. The screenshot's 96% SpO₂ is a direct measurement from another view/time window; it should be displayed with that timestamp and must not be described as unavailable merely because a separate analysis window has no in-window observations. ECG candidate 165 bpm with signal quality 0/100 is correctly withheld as physiological output while retaining raw waveform for research.

**Research evidence used to set scope:** Banister TRIMP is duration-weighted heart-rate-reserve training load (DOI [10.1152/japplphysiol.00482.2003](https://doi.org/10.1152/japplphysiol.00482.2003)); this supports that specific training-load method, not Xiaomi sensor calibration. A systematic review of wrist actigraphy sleep-stage classification found few heterogeneous validation studies and no quantitative pooled estimate ([J Sleep Res. 2024;33:e14143](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC7618292/)). Wearable HRV reviews emphasize protocol/device/measurement-quality dependence ([PMID 29668452](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/29668452/)). Public research datasets may support method benchmarking, but their device/population/task and data-use license must match the claim; they cannot calibrate this watch by themselves.

**Follow-up review — 2026-09-29:** The user challenged the interpretation that missing inputs mean algorithms should not be used. Four internal reviewers re-examined the screenshots and code; no external AI received project or health data. Consensus: algorithms are appropriate and should be developed; the bug is over-coupling a metric to unrelated/missing inputs and burying valid measurements in a dense diagnostic report. One reviewer suspected that an out-of-window SpO₂ record discards all other samples; direct code inspection disproved this for the current revision because `onlyInQueryWindow` retains in-window records before the oxygen summary. Do not implement a duplicate fix for that stale hypothesis.

**Decision:** do not remove algorithms wholesale. Continue formula-based outputs with explicit type/provenance: measured (source + measurement time), formula-derived (formula/version + inputs/units), or experimental estimate (coverage + confidence/limitations). Missing data remains unknown, never zero or a normal-value imputation. Show every independently valid metric even if a different composite score cannot be computed. Keep source/overlap gates for sums, but provide per-source values and explain the blocked aggregate. HR 106 is an observed pulse, not resting HR; ECG-derived RR/pulse candidates from quality 0/100 are not usable physiology. A direct SpO₂ observation (e.g. 96%) must display its timestamp/window, not conflict with a separate empty query window. Avoid claiming medical diagnosis or device clinical calibration.

**Next implementation slice:** (1) audit why metric-detail pages hide source-specific values when aggregate integrity rejects summing; (2) replace raw package-ID/exception-dump presentation with concise per-metric states and expandable technical provenance; (3) add tests proving valid observations survive missing unrelated inputs, while overlapping sources remain uncombined; (4) only re-enable/add a composite after formula and minimum-input contract are documented and tested. Relevant evidence: wrist actigraphy sleep-stage methods are heterogeneous and typically require statistical models benchmarked to PSG ([systematic review](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC7618292/)); wearable stress studies use context/personalization and still describe further validation needs ([personalized stress monitoring study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/34892791/)); Banister TRIMP uses session duration and heart-rate reserve and its canonical derivation specifies measured resting and maximal HR ([J Appl Physiol](https://journals.physiology.org/doi/full/10.1152/japplphysiol.00482.2003)). These support using algorithms, but do not validate this watch's sensors or every product score.

**Implementation order:** first audit every displayed metric against its exact formula and source (and remove unsupported clinical-sounding names); next split measured values from composite scores, allow valid partial-period descriptive totals with explicit observed coverage, and display per-source values without cross-source summation; then replace unsupported sleep-stage and stress heuristics with traceable, appropriately labelled methods or remove only those unsupported scores; finally add calculation + UI regression tests for missing days, sparse interval coverage, mixed sources, out-of-window measurements, and quality rejection. The goal is useful, evidence-backed algorithmic output—not zero blanks through imputation.

**Reviewer QA acceptance:** assert value, provenance/window/coverage, and state/reason together. Test sparse-but-valid daily data; never equate missing days with zero or require continuous full-day coverage for a metric that does not require it. Verify that one missing composite input does not hide other estimates, and that measured HR/SpO₂ are not relabelled as rest/estimated values.

### 2026-09-29 evidence decision: unsupported ANS composites and calorie coverage gate

- Two internal reviewers (Health Connect/data and exercise physiology) independently inspected `PhysiologyAlgorithms.ans()` and its callers. The age-derived fixed RMSSD expectation, fixed 60 bpm HR reference, hand-set score weights, and 900/2500 kcal weekly activity-calorie bands have no traceable validation source in this repository. Weekly activity calories are not Banister TRIMP. Requiring every ActiveCaloriesBurned interval to continuously cover all 24 hours of all seven days also mismatches sparse interval record semantics and does not prove measurement completeness.
- Decision before implementation: stop emitting stress/body-battery/recovery/readiness composite numbers and stress-category advice from that untraceable heuristic; stop calling active-calorie variability “training monotony/strain.” Preserve individually valid HRV/RHR/SpO₂ observations, their timestamp/source, personal descriptive trends, and separately eligible exercise-session TRIMP. Do not substitute unknown activity load with LOW or zero. Replace misleading “insufficient data” copy for disabled scores with the actual reason: no traceable, validated composite model.
- Acceptance: test that health-insight generation emits no heuristic stress/body-battery/recovery/readiness numbers or derived advice, while retaining valid single-metric observations and HRV personal trend; test that TRIMP remains independent and input-gated; ensure activity-calorie records remain visible as observations and never become a training monotony score. Run full mobile/Wear unit tests and both release assemblies/lint after the shared insight-model change.
- This is a model-governance correction, not a clinical validation result. Reintroduce any composite only with a named reproducible method, documented inputs/coefficients, justified missingness/baseline rules, and suitable validation evidence.
